Projected gradient iteration:
1. Compute c=convolution(y,y) via FFT.
2. H=max c. Track best.

Optimizer details:
```
def optimize_sequence(y, n, budget):
  best = evaluate(y) (score = 2*maxconv/n)
  H = maxconv
  eta = 0.05? gradient scale: g max maybe 2*max y ~? if y values can be high if initial sharp. Normalize y sum n, typical 1. eta_start maybe 0.2 / (max(abs(g))?)
  momentum m=0
  for iter while time < budget:
     c=convolve(y)
     H=max(c)
     if 2H/n < best_score: update
     if H < 0.75*n*? maybe break.
     # weights: c_pos = c + eps? We use peak weights p = topK or softmax
     # To avoid c negative? conv nonnegative because y nonnegative so c≥0. Use c.
     # weights = (c/H)^p, normalize. p=10-100 depending phase.
     # gradient g = 2 * cross_corr(y, weights)
     # subtract mean and small projection? 
     # momentum: m = beta*m + (1-beta)*g_dir
     # direction = -m - mean? 
     # line search: for trial_eta in [eta, eta*0.5...]:
        z = y + trial_eta * dir? If dir = -m, ensure mean zero? Projection handles.
        new = simplex_project(z)
        Hnew = maxconv(new)
        if Hnew < H*(1-1e-8): accept; H; break
     # if no accept, eta*=0.5; every some iters perturb new by random small direction and try.
     # adaptive p: increase p when H stuck? Start p=5, increase to 20, then maybe.
```
But projection of y + dir: If dir = -g, z = y - η g. Since g components O(1), η maybe 0.1. Momentum can accumulate O(iters), need normalize direction to have typical magnitude ~1 and step maybe 0.1. Use RMS normalization: dir = -m / std(m) * maybe; line search step absolute. Or use g with scale S = max(abs(g)); dir = m/S; step s in [0,1] times y scale? But gradient scale can be large if y sharp. Better: direction as raw -g, step η with backtracking; g typical 2. Use η initial 0.05.

Simplex projection: We can implement exact:
```
def project_simplex(z, target):
  z=np.asarray(z)
  u=np.sort(z)[::-1]
  css=np.cumsum(u)
  rho=np.where(np.arange(1,len(z)+1)*(u - css/np.arange(...)) >0 )
  # standard
```
Could be O(n log n). Simpler clip+normalize:
```
z -= np.mean(z)
z = np.clip(z,0,None)
z *= target / z.sum()

Convolution via FFT:
```
def fft_convolve_nonneg(a):
    n=a.size
    L=1
    while L < 2*n-1: L <<=1
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, L)  # real FFT
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, L)[:2*n-1]
    # due to numerical small negative? c from nonneg should nonnegative, but roundoff; clip max(0)
    return np.maximum(c,0.0)
```

Simplex projection: implement exact and efficient. Need target small? target n.
```
def project_to_simplex(v, target=1.0):
    v=np.asarray(v, dtype=float)
    # If all finite
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    css=np.cumsum(u)
    k=np.arange(1, v.size+1, dtype=float)
    cond = u - css/k > 0
    idx = np.where(cond)[0]
    if idx.size==0:
       # all? use mean?
       gamma = np.mean(v)
    else:
       rho = idx[-1]
       theta = (css[rho] - target)/(rho+1)  # note css index 0-based, rho 0-based => count rho+1
    x = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    # Due to numerical, adjust sum exactly:
    s=x.sum()
    if s <=0: x[:]=target/v.size; return x
    x *= target/s
    return x
```
Need target float.
This sorting each projection. Could use clip+normalize for speed. Exact projection more stable. Use maybe threshold: if n <= 16384, sorting okay. We'll use exact. But line search tries several projections; sorting 4*8k=32k per iter, 1000 iters 32M elements sorted total; maybe okay (0.1s per 1000? Actually sorting 8k ~0.5ms, 4k projections ~1ms, negligible).

```
def smooth_zero_mean(rng, n, beta):
    f = np.fft.rfftfreq(n)
    spec = rng.normal(size=n//2+1) + 1j*rng.normal(size=n//2+1)
    mask = f>0
    spec[mask] /= (1+ (f[n]*np.sqrt(mask)) )**beta? # lowpass
    spec[0]=0; if even spec[-1]=0? for anti? Not necessary.
    z = np.fft.irfft(spec, n)
    z -= z.mean()
    z /= (np.std(z)+1e-12)
    return z
```

def optimize_runtime(y0, n, L, phase, deadline, rng, p_start=4.0, p_end=16.0, eta0=0.12, max_seconds=None, target_score=1.49, init_best_score=None):
    y = np.array(y0, dtype=float)
    y = clip_normalize(y,n)
    H = max_conv_from_fft(y,L)
    score = 2*H/n
    best_y=y.copy(); best_score=score
    eta=eta0
    m=np.zeros(n)
    beta_mom=0.85
    stuck=0; improve_streak=0; it=0
    last_improve=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       # If score near target, we can continue maybe lower; break if score < 1.2? Maybe use remaining for other lengths; return.
       p = p_start + (p_end-p_start)*min(1.0, it/2000)
       c, F = compute_c_and_F? We need F for gradient. Use `c, F = conv_fft_F(y,L)`.
       H = c.max(); score = 2*H/n
       if score < best_score - 1e-12: best...
       if not finite or H<=0: break
       # weights and grad
       w = make_weights(c,p)
       g = correlation_weighted(y, F, w, L, phase, n) # include factor 2
       # Remove mean? g_mean = g.mean(); g = g - g_mean? Projection does, but removing can reduce constant momentum.
       g = g - g.mean()
       if not finite: random
       m = beta_mom*m + (1-beta_mom)*g
       direction = -m
       # normalize direction to typical size 1:
       rms = np.sqrt(np.mean(direction*direction))
       if rms <=1e-12: random
       direction = direction / rms
       # step size: want change scale maybe 0.2 of y mean. y_scale = max(1.0, np.median(y[y>0]))? Use 1.
       trial = eta
       improved=False
       for _ in range(6):
           # line search maybe try z = y + trial*direction (raw change). Direction normalized to rms 1, so trial absolute y units.
           z = y + trial*direction
           z_np = np.clip(z,0,None)
           # projection target n
           new_y = project_simplex(z_np, n)  # exact includes nonneg
           # if too close? 
           Hnew = max_conv_from_fft(new_y, L)
           snew = 2*Hnew/n
           if snew < score - 5e-6:
               y=new_y; H=Hnew; score=snew; improved=True
               if snew < best_score: best...
               eta = min(eta*1.15, 2.0)
               break
           # maybe accept if snew < best_score? If current not improving but best improves, accept? To continue from best? We can set y to new if snew < best_score - 1e-7 even if current? Use current H; but if snew between current and best? current is best maybe. Need accept if snew < score for local; else if snew < best_score (unlikely). Maybe accept if snew < score or snew < best_score? We keep y current.
           trial *= 0.5
       if improved:
          stuck=0; last_improve=time
       else:
          # no descent: update eta smaller, random period
          eta = max(eta*0.5, 1e-5)
          stuck +=1
          if stuck % 20 ==0 or time - last_improve > 10:
             # random perturb around y or best_y
             if rng.random()<0.5:
                y = add_random_direction(y, n, rng, amp=0.1+0.4*rng.random())
             else:
                y = best_y.copy()
                y = add_random_direction(y, n, rng, amp=0.2+0.6*rng.random())
             H=max_conv_from_fft(y,L); score=2*H/n
             if score < best_score: best...
             project
             eta=eta0
       # If score < 1.0? no
       if score < 1.2: break? But target 1.5, maybe continue a bit; if very low, we can break to save time. Set if score < 1.35 break outer? Not necessarily. Could break if score <1.45? It may still improve, but time for other starts. We can allow break if score < 1.40. Not sure. We'll set target_stop_score = min(1.45, target-0.05?) Actually target 1.5027; if score <1.42, likely good; break to use time. But a single length won't fully confirm. Use parameter `stop_score=1.45`.
```

Potential speedup: Use `np.fft.rfft` for max. Let's implement max with rfft for speed:
```
def max_convolution(y, L, n):
    fy = np.fft.rfft(y, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fy*fy, L)[:2*n-1]
    return float(np.max(c))
```
That is faster. For c+F we need complex F for gradient. Could compute F complex and c with ifft. Maybe okay. We can use complex everywhere for speed consistency? rfft c only for line search; gradient uses complex. But code complexity okay. Now weights p: Since c scale H, c/H. If H==0. Use `pn = np.power(cn, p)`; if p large and cn<1 underflow to zero at many; okay. But sum could underflow if only max: use if sum==0 set weights one-hot top. Implementation:
```
def make_weights(c, p):
   H=c.max()
   if H <=0: return zeros
   cn = c/H
   # To avoid underflow, use logs? For p up to 64, fine: min positive maybe 1e-20, underflow if p=100. Use p<=32.
   w = cn**p
   s=w.sum()
   if s<=1e-300: 
      idx=np.argmax(c); w[idx]=1
   else: w/=s
   return w
```
Maybe add small floor: `w += 1e-18` to avoid all zeros. But weights sum normalized; if floor too high, includes flat. Use p schedule <=16.

Momentum and direction: If we normalize direction by RMS, momentum scale irrelevant after normalization; but direction still direction. If m direction changes, okay. Use `m = 0.9*m + 0.1*g`; then dir = -m; dir = dir/rms. If gradient direction has small RMS due to near stationary, random if <1e-8.

Initial `smooth_noise`: implement using rfft.
```
def smooth_noise(rng, n, beta):
    freq = np.fft.rfftfreq(n)
    spec = rng.normal(size=n//2+1) + 1j*rng.normal(size=n//2+1)
    nz = freq > 0
    scale = np.ones_like(freq)
    scale[nz] = (1.0 / (1.0 + 8.0 * freq[nz]))**beta  # lowpass
    spec *= scale
    spec[0]=0
    if n%2==0: spec[-1]=0? Nyquist for real if we want zero mean; set.
    z = np.fft.irfft(spec, n)
    z -= z.mean()
    std=z.std()
    if std>1e-12: z/=std
    else: z=rng.normal(size=n); z-=z.mean()
    return z
```
For antisym: `z - z[::-1]` then normalize. Note if n odd, middle zero. Sum zero automatically? z - z[::-1] sum = sum z - sum z =0 yes.

Normalize function: 
```
def normalize_to_sum(x, target):
  x=np.asarray(x,float)
  x=np.where(np.isfinite(x),x,0)
  x=np.maximum(x,0)
  x=np.minimum(x,1000.0)
  s=x.sum()
  if s <= 1e-12: x=np.ones(x.size); s=x.size
  x*=target/s
  x=np.minimum(x,1000.0); s=x.sum(); x*=target/s
  return x
```
If clipping creates sum maybe, second.

Pseudo:
```
def optimize_runtime(y0, n, L, phase, max_seconds, rng, p_start=4.0, p_end=20.0, eta0=0.2, stop_score=1.45, give_up_seconds=20.0):
    y = normalize_to_sum(y0, n)
    c, F = conv_and_F(y, L, n)
    H = float(c.max()); score=2*H/n
    best_y=y.copy(); best_score=score
    eta=eta0
    m=np.zeros(n)
    last_improve=time.monotonic()
    it=0
    start_time=time.monotonic()
    while time.monotonic() - start_time < max_seconds:
        if time.monotonic() - last_improve > give_up_seconds: break
        if score < stop_score: break
        # compute fresh maybe if y updated in previous accept, already have c,F,H; after random need recompute outside.
        # In loop, at top have c,F,H? We'll initialize and update after accept/random.
        w = make_weights(c,p_start+...)
        wpad allocate? Instead in corr function take w and compute FFT.
        g = correlation_weighted(F, w, L, phase, n) # no y needed
        g -= g.mean()
        if not finite: random
        m = 0.7*m + 0.3*g
        direction = -m
        # Add extra from top active lags using c
        if np.random? rng.random()<0.6:
            top_k = min(3, c.size)
            idx = np.argpartition(c, top_k-1)[-top_k:] # if top_k=1, argpartition? handle
            extra = np.zeros(n)
            for kk in idx:
                k=int(kk)
                lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
                i=np.arange(lo,hi+1)
                j=k-i
                mask=i<j
                ii=i[mask]; jj=j[mask]
                diff=y[jj]-y[ii]
                extra[ii] += diff
                extra[jj] -= diff
            direction += 0.5*extra
        # optional add tiny random direction to avoid exact stagnation
        # if direction norm zero or stuck: random
        rms = np.sqrt(np.mean(np.square(direction)))
        if not np.isfinite(rms) or rms < 1e-12:
            direction = smooth_noise(rng,n,1.5); direction -= direction.mean()
        else:
            direction = direction / rms
        # line search
        trial=eta
        improved=False
        for _ in range(6):
            z = y + trial*direction
            new_y = project_simplex(z, n)
            # if new_y nearly same? 
            Hnew = max_convolution(new_y,n,L) # uses rfft; or compute c? For accept, enough.
            snew = 2*Hnew/n
            if snew < score - 1e-9:
                y = new_y; H=Hnew; score=snew
                if snew < best_score:
                    best_score=snew; best_y=y.copy()
                    last_improve=time.monotonic()
                improved=True
                eta = min(eta*1.15, 1.0)
                break
            trial *=0.5
        if improved:
            # recompute c,F for next iteration? Need c and F for weights. We can compute c,F now.
            c, F = conv_and_F(y,L,n)
            # ensure H matches c.max maybe; use c.max
            H2=c.max(); if H2 < H? update score? Due to rfft vs ifft small. Use c.
            H=H2; score=2*H/n; update best.
            stuck_iters=0
        else:
            stuck_iters +=1
            eta = max(eta*0.5, 1e-5)
            if stuck_iters > 30 or time.monotonic()-last_improve > 5.0:
                # random perturbation
                amp = 0.1 + 0.5*rng.random()
                # choose base y or best_y
                if rng.random() < 0.3:
                    y = best_y.copy()
                y = add_random_direction(y, n, rng, amp)
                y = project_simplex(y,n)
                y, c, F, H, score = recompute
                if score < best_score: update best
                else: last_improve? If random doesn't improve but gives new state, we can allow some no-improve time; reset last_improve if score < score_before? We didn't store. We'll set last_improve only if best improves; else if random state score < previous score? We can compare before random.
```

Potential speed: In each iteration, to compute g, we need `W=fft(wpad)`. Allocating wpad each time costly. Preallocate `wpad` inside optimize and fill:

But `np.fft.fft` may copy; okay. We can pass function `correlation_from_F(F, w, wpad, L, phase,n)` to fill and FFT. Top-level with wpad param.

```
def correlation_weighted(F, w, wpad, L, phase, n):
    wpad[:len(w)] = w
    wpad[len(w):] = 0.0
    W = np.fft.fft(wpad, L)  # actually if pass length L it uses full length; wpad length L.
    Frev = np.conj(F) * phase
    C = np.fft.ifft(Frev * W)
    return 2.0 * C[n-1:2*n-1].real
```
But F length L, wpad length L. `np.fft.fft(wpad)` no length needed. `phase` length L. Good.

In `conv_and_F`, if using complex FFT: `F=np.fft.fft(y,L)` (y length n). It zero pads. `C=np.fft.ifft(F*F)`. Use c.

Line search `max_convolution(y,n,L)` with rfft:
```
def max_convolution(y,n,L):
    fy=np.fft.rfft(y,L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fy*fy,L)[:2*n-1]
    return float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
```
Could use `np.fft.rfft(y, L)` each trial. Good.

Projection function: We can use exact but target is n. Sorting expensive but okay. Implement with `float(target)` and `v.size`.
```
def project_simplex(v, target):
    n=v.size
    if n==0: return v
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    css=np.cumsum(u)
    k=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=float)
    cond = u - css/k > 0
    idx=np.where(cond)[0]
    if idx.size==0:
        theta = (u.sum()-target)/n? If none positive, all below? Standard: rho=0? Let's handle.
    else:
        rho=idx[-1]
        theta=(css[rho]-target)/(rho+1)
    x=np.maximum(v-theta,0.0)
    s=x.sum()
    if s <= 1e-12: x=np.full(n,target/n); return x
    x *= target/s
    return x
```
If cond empty (all u - css/k <=0) means even largest not >? This happens if target >= sum? But target may equal sum of v? For v maybe sum >target? If target small? We can fallback: `theta = np.sum(v)-target/n`? Let's derive if no index, set gamma = (sum(v)-target)/n? Standard if no positive, then all zeros? But target positive. E.g., v all negative large, target positive; sorting negative, cond false. Then x=0, sum0, fallback uniform target/n. Fine.

Maybe use `project_simplex` with v that may not finite. Ensure finite before.

`add_random_direction`: It may create all zeros? handle.
```
def add_random_direction(y,n,rng,amp):
    beta = 1.0 + 2.0*rng.random()
    u = smooth_noise(rng,n,beta)
    if rng.random() < 0.5:
        u = u - u[::-1]
    # optional clamp amp
    z = y + amp*u
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, y)
    return project_simplex(z,n)
```
`smooth_noise` returns finite.

`make_initial(y, n, rng, base, kind, amp)`:
```
def make_initial(n,rng,base,kind,amp):
   if base is not None and kind==0:
       y=resize_to_n(base,n); return normalize_to_sum(y,n)
   if base is not None and kind in (1,2):
       y=resize_to_n(base,n)
       u=smooth_noise(...)
       if kind==2 and rng.random()<.5: u=u-u[::-1]
       y = y + amp*u
       return project_simplex(normalize_to_sum(y,n),n)
   # kind others
   y=np.ones(n)
   if kind==3: # smooth noise
      u=smooth_noise(rng,n,1.0+1.5*rng.random())
      if rng.random()<0.5: u=u-u[::-1]
      y=(1.0+amp*u)
   elif kind==4: # blocks
      R=int(8*(2**rng.integers(0,4)))? Ensure top-level no lambda.
      heights=np.exp(rng.normal(size=R, scale=0.5)) +0.2
      # block sizes uneven? tile/resize
      y=np.interp(np.linspace(0,R, n, endpoint=False), np.linspace(0,R,R,endpoint=False), heights)
   elif kind==5: # multi-sine
      y=np.ones(n)
      x=np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=False)
      for _ in range(3):
          m=int(rng.integers(1,12))
          phase=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
          wa=rng.uniform(-1,1)
          y += amp*wa*np.sin(2*np.pi*m*x+phase)
   elif kind==6: # raw random lowpass with larger amp
      u=smooth_noise(std ... beta low)
      y=(1+amp*u)
   y=np.maximum(y,0)
   return project_simplex(normalize_to_sum(y,n),n)
```

`next_fft_length(n, multiplier=3)`:
```
def next_fft_length(n):
    need = 3*n - 2
    if need < 1: need=1
    L=1
    while L < need: L <<= 1
    return L
```
For max convolution, L must at least 2n-1; 3n okay. If n small, L maybe 4.

`make_phase(n,L)`:
```
def make_phase(n,L):
    return np.exp(-2j*np.pi*(n-1)*np.arange(L)/float(L))
```

`normalize_to_sum`:
```
def normalize_to_sum(x,target):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=float)
    if x.ndim !=1:
       x=x.ravel()
    x=np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x=np.maximum(x,0.0)
    x=np.minimum(x, MAX_VALUE)
    s=float(x.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
       x=np.ones(x.size, dtype=float)
       s=float(x.size)
    x = x * (float(target)/s)
    x=np.minimum(x, MAX_VALUE)
    s=float(x.sum())
    if s <=1e-15:
       x=np.ones(x.size); s=float(x.size)
    x = x * (float(target)/s)
    return x
```
If target n int. Good.

`resize_to_n`:
```
def resize_to_n(seq,n):
    seq=np.asarray(seq,dtype=float).ravel()
    seq=np.where(np.isfinite(seq),seq,0.0)
    m=seq.size
    if m==0: return np.ones(n)
    if m==n: return seq
    old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m,endpoint=False) if m>1 else [0.0]
    new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n,endpoint=False)
    return np.interp(new, old, seq)
```
If m large, linspace memory okay. For m=1, handle.

   n=int(y.size)
   if n<1: return np.inf
   y=normalize_to_sum(y,n)
   if y.sum() < 0.01? target n so sum=n so ok.
   L=next_fft_length(n)
   H=max_convolution(y,n,L)
   return float(2.0*H/float(n))
```

`max_convolution`:
```
def max_convolution(y,n,L):
   if n<=0: return 0.0
   fy=np.fft.rfft(y,L)
   cf=np.fft.irfft(fy*fy,L)
   c=cf[:2*n-1]
   if c.size==0: return 0.0
   return float(np.max(c))
```
Clip negative? max may be tiny negative if all? But y nonnegative, should nonnegative. If due to -1e-15, max could negative only if all zero; sum n => no. Use `max(float(np.max(c)),0.0)`.

`conv_and_F(y,n,L)`:
```
def conv_and_F(y,n,L):
   F=np.fft.fft(y,L)
   C=np.fft.ifft(F*F)
   c=C[:2*n-1].real
   # ensure nonnegative
   c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
   return c,F
```
Could use `np.fft.irfft` if we store F? We use ifft. For real, ifft returns complex with tiny imag. Good.

def make_weights(c,p,top_blend=0.35):
    H=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    m=c.size
    if H<=1e-300 or m==0:
       w=np.ones(m,dtype=float); return w/m
    cn=c/H
    # Replace 0? no
    w=np.power(cn, float(p))
    s=float(w.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
       idx=int(np.argmax(c)); w=np.zeros(m); w[idx]=1.0; return w
    w /= s
    if top_blend !=0:
       topk = min(10, m)
       if topk >1:
          idx=np.argpartition(c, topk-1)[-topk:]
       else:
          idx=np.array([int(np.argmax(c))])
       wtop=np.zeros(m,dtype=float)
       wtop[idx]=1.0/topk
       w=(1.0-top_blend)*w + top_blend*wtop
       s=float(w.sum())
       if s>0: w/=s
    return w
```

def correlation_weighted(F,w,wpad,L,phase,n):
    m=w.size
    wpad[:m]=w
    wpad[m:]=0.0
    W=np.fft.fft(wpad)
    Frev=np.conj(F)*phase
    C=np.fft.ifft(Frev*W)
    return 2.0*C[n-1:2*n-1].real
```

`project_simplex` exact. Need target int maybe but target can float. Use `n = v.size`. If v has NaN? maybe return uniform. We'll ensure finite.
```
def project_simplex(v,target):
    n=v.size
    v=np.where(np.isfinite(v),v,0.0)
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    css=np.cumsum(u)
    k=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=float)
    cond = u - css/k > 0.0
    idx=np.where(cond)[0]
    if idx.size > 0:
        rho=int(idx[-1])
        theta=(float(css[rho]) - float(target))/(rho+1.0)
    else:
        theta = (float(np.sum(v)) - float(target))/float(n) if n>0 else 0.0
    x=np.maximum(v-theta,0.0)
    s=float(x.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
       x=np.full(n, float(target)/n if n else 0.0)
       return x
    x *= float(target)/s
    # ensure target exact and nonnegative
    x=np.maximum(x,0.0)
    s2=float(x.sum())
    if s2 > 0:
       x *= float(target)/s2
    return x
```
The else theta maybe not standard but fallback.

`smooth_noise`:
```
def smooth_noise(rng,n,beta=1.5):
    if n <= 1: return np.zeros(n)
    freq=np.fft.rfftfreq(n)
    size=n//2+1
    spec=(rng.normal(size=size)+1j*rng.normal(size=size)).astype(np.complex128)
    nz=freq>1e-15
    scale=np.ones(size,dtype=float)
    # cutoff based on max frequency 0.5
    scale[nz]=(1.0/(1.0+16.0*freq[nz]))**float(beta)
    spec*=scale
    spec[0]=0.0
    if size>1 and n%2==0:
       spec[-1]=0.0
    z=np.fft.irfft(spec,n)
    z-=z.mean()
    std=float(np.std(z))
    if std <=1e-12:
       z=rng.normal(size=n)
       z-=z.mean()
       std=float(np.std(z))
    if std > 1e-12:
       z/=std
    return z
```
Maybe cutoff 16 freq too low; for beta low retains high? We want smooth lowpass. Use `1.0/(1.0+ (freq/(0.05))**2)`? Let's use `cutoff=0.15` (cycles/sample). `scale[nz]=np.exp(-(freq[nz]/0.10)**beta)`? Simpler:
`scale[nz]=1/(1+(freq[nz]/0.08)**2)**(beta/2)`. This smooth. We'll implement:
```
sig=0.03+0.06*rng.random()? beta variable.
scale[nz]=np.exp(-0.5*(freq[nz]/sig)**2)
```
But function signature beta maybe not. Use beta as inverse smoothness: `sig = 0.02 + 0.08 * beta`? Let's define `smooth_noise(rng,n,smooth=1.0)` where larger smoother. `sig=0.02+0.05*smooth`. Then scale=exp(-0.5*(freq/sig)^2). For smooth=1 sig0.07. Good. We'll adjust calls.
```
def smooth_noise(rng,n,smooth=1.0):
    sig=0.02+0.05*max(0.1,float(smooth))
    ... scale[nz]=np.exp(-0.5*(freq[nz]/sig)**2)
```
This is lowpass Gaussian. Good.

`antisym_noise` not separate; in caller.

`block_perturb`:
```
def block_perturb(y,n,rng,sigma=None):
   if n<3: return y.copy()
   if sigma is None: sigma=0.15+0.35*rng.random()
   cand=y.copy()
   max_width=max(3,n//8)
   min_width=max(2,min(max_width,n//64) if n//64>=2 else 2)
   width=int(rng.integers(min_width, max_width+1))
   start=int(rng.integers(0, max(1,n-width+1)))
   # choose lognormal factor
   factor=float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,sigma)))
   cand[start:start+width]*=factor
   if rng.random()<0.25:
       # second smaller block
       width2=max(2,width//3)
       start2=int(rng.integers(0,max(1,n-width2+1)))
       factor2=float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,sigma)))
       cand[start2:start2+width2]*=factor2
   return normalize_to_sum(cand,n)
```
No lambda. Use `rng.integers(low, high)` high exclusive. Need ensure min_width < max_width+1. Handle small n.

`make_initial`:
```
def make_initial(n,rng,base,kind,amp):
    if base is not None and kind < 2:
       y=resize_to_n(base,n)
       if kind==0:
          return normalize_to_sum(y,n)
       # kind 1 base+noise
       u=smooth_noise(rng,n, smooth=0.5+1.5*rng.random())
       if rng.random()<0.5: u=u-u[::-1]
       y=np.asarray(y)*float(amp) + u? Actually y base normalized sum n; y + amp*u (u std1)
       y = y + amp*u
       return project_simplex(normalize_to_sum(y,n), n)
    y=np.ones(n,dtype=float)
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n,endpoint=False)
    if kind==2:
       u=smooth_noise(rng,n, smooth=0.5+2*rng.random())
       if rng.random()<0.5: u=u-u[::-1]
       y += amp*u
    elif kind==3:
       R=int(8 << rng.integers(0,4))  # 8,16,32,64
       heights=np.exp(rng.normal(loc=0.0, scale=0.5, size=R)) + 0.3
       # interpolate block heights to n
       old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,R,endpoint=False)
       y = np.interp(x, old, heights)
    elif kind==4:
       # sine multi
       for _ in range(3):
           m=int(rng.integers(1,16))
           ph=float(rng.uniform(0.0,2*np.pi))
           wa=float(rng.uniform(-1.0,1.0))
           y += amp*wa*np.sin(2*np.pi*m*x+ph)
    elif kind==5:
       u=smooth_noise(rng,n, smooth=0.8+0.8*rng.random())
       if rng.random()<0.5: u=(u - u.max()) + ( - (u - u[::-1])?) no
       y += amp*u
    elif kind==6:
       # random lowpass with heavy amplitude then soft plus maybe exponent
       u=smooth_noise(rng,n, smooth=0.3+1.7*rng.random())
       y *= np.exp(amp*u)  # multiplicative
    else:
       # given handles; fallback random uniform
       pass
    y=np.maximum(y,0.0)
    return project_simplex(normalize_to_sum(y,n), n)
```
Need in kind 3, `np.interp` requires old array length R. Good. R power shift; `rng.integers(0,4)` returns 0..3. `8 <<`? In Python, `8 << rng.integers(0,4)` returns int? Actually int 8 left shift numpy int works? Use `R = 8 * (2 ** int(rng.integers(0,4)))`. Good.

Kind 6 multiplicative may amplify; normalize. Good.

`add_random_direction`:
```
def add_random_direction(y,n,rng,amp):
    r=rng.random()
    if r <0.3:
       return block_perturb(y,n,rng)
    u=smooth_noise(rng,n, smooth=0.3+1.7*rng.random())
    if rng.random()<0.5: u=u-u[::-1] else maybe u = u + 0.5*u? no.
    z=np.asarray(y)+amp*u
    z=np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, y)
    return project_simplex(z,n)
```

`optimize_runtime` details with time. It needs `max_seconds`, `deadline`? Use start + max_seconds, but also global deadline? We can pass `hard_deadline` to not overrun. Use `end_time = min(start_time+max_seconds, hard_deadline)`. Helper:
```
def optimize_runtime(y0,n,L,phase,hard_deadline,max_seconds,rng,p_start=4.0,p_end=20.0,eta0=0.2,stop_score=1.40,give_up_seconds=20.0):
    start=time.monotonic(); end=min(start+max_seconds, hard_deadline)
    ... while time.monotonic() < end ...
```
Return best_y,best_score. It also may return if `score < stop_score`? We'll set stop_score maybe 1.35 to keep searching; but if time for task is split, stopping early okay. If best below 1.5, maybe stop at 1.45. We can pass stop_score dynamically. If global best <1.4, stop lower 1.3.

Let's define `run_optimizer` with parameters.

Important: In main iteration, c,F from previous y. After random, recompute. We need function `recompute` inline.
Pseudo code in optimize:
```
def optimize_runtime(...):
    y=normalize_to_sum(y0,n)
    c,F=conv_and_F(y,n,L)
    H=float(c.max()); score=2*H/n
    best_y=y.copy(); best_score=score
    m=np.zeros(n)
    wpad=np.zeros(L)
    eta=eta_start
    stuck=0
    last_progress=start
    p_idx=0
    p_cycle=[4.0,6.0,8.0,10.0,14.0,18.0,24.0]
    while time.monotonic() < end:
        if time.monotonic() - last_progress > max(give_up_seconds, 5.0): break
        if score < stop_score: break
        p = p_cycle[min(p_idx, len-1)]
        p_idx = (p_idx+1)%len(p_cycle)
        w=make_weights(c,p)
        g=correlation_weighted(F,w,wpad,L,phase,n)
        g-=g.mean()
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)) or float(np.max(np.abs(g)))<=1e-12:
            # random direction
            direction=smooth_noise(rng,n,smooth=1.0)
            direction-=direction.mean()
        else:
            m = 0.7*m + 0.3*g
            direction=-m
            # add extra
            if rng.random() < 0.7:
                topk = min(3, c.size)
                if topk>0:
                   if topk < c.size:
                      idx=np.argpartition(c, topk-1)[-topk:]
                   else:
                      idx=np.arange(c.size)
                   extra=np.zeros(n)
                   for kk in idx:
                      k=int(kk)
                      lo=max(0, k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
                      if hi>=lo:
                         i=np.arange(lo,hi+1)
                         j=k-i
                         mask=i<j
                         ii=i[mask]; jj=j[mask]
                         diff=y[jj]-y[ii]
                         extra[ii]+=diff; extra[jj]-=diff
                   direction += 0.6*extra
            direction -= direction.mean()
            rms=float(np.sqrt(np.mean(direction*direction)))
            if not np.isfinite(rms) or rms <= 1e-12:
                direction=smooth_noise(rng,n,smooth=1.0); direction-=direction.mean()
            else:
                direction /= rms
        # line search
        trial=eta
        improved=False
        for _ in range(5):
            z=y+trial*direction
            new_y=project_simplex(z,n)
            Hn=max_convolution(new_y,n,L)
            sn=2*Hn/n
            if sn < score - 1e-9:
                y=new_y; H=Hn; score=sn
                if sn < best_score:
                    best_score=sn; best_y=y.copy()
                last_progress=time.monotonic(); improved=True
                eta=min(eta*1.12, 0.9)
                break
            trial *=0.5
        if improved:
            c,F=conv_and_F(y,n,L)
            H2=float(c.max()); score=2*H2/n
            if H2 < H: H=H2; if score<best... update
            stuck=0
        else:
            eta=max(eta*0.5, 1e-4)
            stuck +=1
            if stuck > 20:
                score_before=score
                if rng.random()<0.25:
                   y=best_y.copy()
                y=add_random_direction(y,n,rng,0.15+0.45*rng.random())
                c,F=conv_and_F(y,n,L)
                H=float(c.max()); score=2*H/n
                if score < score_before or score < best_score:
                    if score < best_score: best...
                    last_progress=time.monotonic()
                stuck=0
                eta=eta_start
    return best_y,best_score
```

Detailed code for `optimize_runtime`:
```
def optimize_runtime(y0, n, L, phase, hard_deadline, max_seconds, rng, eta_start=0.20, stop_score=1.42, give_up_seconds=20.0):
    start = time.monotonic()
    end = min(start + max(1.0, max_seconds), hard_deadline)
    y = normalize_to_sum(y0, n)
    c,F = conv_and_F(y,n,L)
    H = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    score = 2.0*H/float(n)
    best_y=y.copy(); best_score=score
    m=np.zeros(n)
    wpad=np.zeros(L)
    eta=eta_start
    stuck=0
    last_progress=start
    p_cycle = (4.0,6.0,8.0,10.0,13.0,16.0,20.0,26.0)
    p_idx=0
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < end:
       if not np.isfinite(score) or H <= 0.0:
          y=best_y.copy(); c,F=conv...; H=...; score=...
       if time.monotonic() - last_progress > max(2.0, give_up_seconds): break
       if score < stop_score: break
       p = p_cycle[p_idx]; p_idx=(p_idx+1)%len(p_cycle)
       w=make_weights(c,p)
       g=correlation_weighted(F,w,wpad,L,phase,n)
       g=np.asarray(g,dtype=float)
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
          direction=smooth_noise(rng,n,1.0); direction-=direction.mean()
       else:
          g = g - g.mean()
          m = 0.7*m + 0.3*g
          direction = -m
          # extra
          if rng.random() < 0.75 and c.size > 0:
             topk = min(3, c.size)
             if topk > 1 and topk < c.size:
                idx = np.argpartition(c, topk-1)[-topk:]
             elif topk == 1:
                idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))])
             else:
                idx = np.arange(c.size)
             extra = np.zeros(n)
             for kk in idx:
                k=int(kk)
                lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
                if hi >= lo:
                   iarr=np.arange(lo, hi+1)
                   jarr=k-iarr
                   mask=iarr<jarr
                   ii=iarr[mask]; jj=jarr[mask]
                   if ii.size:
                      diff = y[ii] - y[jj]
                      extra[ii] += diff
                      extra[jj] -= diff
             direction += 0.6*extra
          direction = direction - float(np.mean(direction))
          rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(direction*direction)))
          if not np.isfinite(rms) or rms <= 1e-12:
             direction = smooth_noise(rng,n,1.0)
             direction = direction - float(np.mean(direction))
             rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(direction*direction)))
          if np.isfinite(rms) and rms > 1e-12:
             direction /= rms
          else:
             direction = smooth_noise(rng,n,1.0); direction-=direction.mean()
       # line search
       trial = eta
       improved = False
       for _ in range(5):
          z = y + trial*direction
          new_y = project_simplex(z, n)
          Hn = max_convolution(new_y,n,L)
          sn = 2.0*Hn/float(n)
          if np.isfinite(sn) and sn < score - 1e-9:
             y = new_y; H = Hn; score = sn
             if sn < best_score:
                best_score = sn; best_y = y.copy()
             last_progress = time.monotonic()
             improved = True
             eta = min(eta*1.12, 0.9)
             break
          trial *= 0.5
       if improved:
          c,F=conv_and_F(y,n,L)
          H2=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
          score2=2.0*H2/float(n)
          H=H2; score=score2
          if score2 < best_score:
             best_score=score2; best_y=y.copy()
          stuck=0
       else:
          eta = max(eta*0.5, 1e-4)
          stuck +=1
          if stuck > 25 or time.monotonic() - last_progress > 10.0:
             score_before = score
             if rng.random() < 0.25:
                y = best_y.copy()
             y = add_random_direction(y,n,rng,0.15+0.45*float(rng.random()))
             c,F=conv_and_F(y,n,L)
             H=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
             score=2.0*H/float(n)
             if (np.isfinite(score) and (score < score_before or score < best_score)):
                if score < best_score:
                   best_score=score; best_y=y.copy()
                last_progress=time.monotonic()
             m.fill(0.0)
             stuck=0
             eta=eta_start
       it +=1
    return best_y, best_score
```

Detailed `propose_candidate` code:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    """..."""
    budget = float(budget_s)
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 0: budget=30.0
    start = time.monotonic()
    hard_deadline = start + max(2.0, budget - 15.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(1234567)
    if seed is not None:
       try: rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed)+1)
       except: rng = np.random.default_rng(1234567)
    best_y = None
    best_score = float('inf')
    g = get_global_height()
    base_seq = None
    if g is not None:
       try:
          arr = np.asarray(g, dtype=float).ravel()
          arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
          if arr.size > 0:
             if arr.size > 12000:
                base_seq = resize_to_n(arr, 6121)
                base_n = 6121
             elif arr.size < 128:
                base_seq = resize_to_n(arr, 256)
                base_n = 256
             else:
                base_seq = arr.copy()
                base_n = arr.size
             base_seq = normalize_to_sum(base_seq, base_n)
             s = 2.0 * max_convolution(base_seq, base_n, next_fft_length(base_n)) / float(base_n)
             if np.isfinite(s):
                best_y = base_seq.copy(); best_score = float(s)
       except Exception:
          base_seq = None
    if best_y is None:
       base_seq = None
    # phases
    raw=[]
    if base_seq is not None and base_seq.size not in (512,1024,2048,4096,6121):
       raw.append((base_seq.size, 0.08, 1))
    for n, frac in [(512,0.24),(1024,0.24),(2048,0.18),(4096,0.14),(6121,0.12)]:
       if not any(p[0]==n for p in raw):
          raw.append((n, frac))
    total_frac = sum(f for _,f in raw)
    available = max(60.0, hard_deadline - time.monotonic() - 15.0)
    phase_work = available * 0.9
    for n, frac in raw:
       phase_budget = (frac/total_frac)*phase_work
       phase_budget = min(phase_budget, max(0.0, hard_deadline - time.monotonic() - 10.0))
       if phase_budget < 15.0: break
       L, ph = get_fft_params(n)
       starts = max(1, int(phase_budget / 35.0))
       starts = min(starts, 12)
       per_start = phase_budget / float(starts)
       if base_seq is not None:
          task_base = resize_to_n(base_seq, n)
          task_base = normalize_to_sum(task_base, n)
       else:
          task_base = None
       for si in range(starts):
          if time.monotonic() > hard_deadline - 10.0: break
          if task_base is not None and si == 0: kind = 0
          elif task_base is not None and si == 1: kind = 1
          else: kind = 2 + (si % 5)
          amp = 0.25 + 0.25*((si % 5)/4.0) + 0.15*float(rng.random())
          y0 = make_initial(n, rng, task_base, kind, amp)
          stop_s = 1.40 if best_score < 1.5027 else 1.46
          give = min(20.0, max(5.0, 0.6*per_start))
          max_sec = min(per_start + 5.0, max(5.0, hard_deadline - time.monotonic() - 10.0))
          yb, sb = optimize_runtime(y0, n, L, ph, hard_deadline, max_sec, rng, stop_score=stop_s, give_up_seconds=give)
          if np.isfinite(sb) and sb < best_score:
             best_score = float(sb)
             best_y = yb.copy()
             print(f"[search] n={n} start={si} local_score={sb:.6f} best={best_score:.6f} time_left={hard_deadline-time.monotonic():.1f}", flush=True)
    # extra
    if best_y is not None:
       remaining = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
       if remaining > 45.0:
          nb = int(best_y.size)
          Lb, phb = get_fft_params(nb)
          stop_s = 1.32 if best_score < 1.45 else 1.38
          max_sec = min(remaining * 0.55, 360.0)
          yb, sb = optimize_runtime(best_y, nb, Lb, phb, hard_deadline, max_sec, rng, stop_score=stop_s, give_up_seconds=30.0)
          if np.isfinite(sb) and sb < best_score:
             best_score=float(sb); best_y=xb? yb.copy(); print
       remaining = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
       if best_y is not None and remaining > 90.0:
          nb = int(best_y.size)
          tried=False
          for n_up in (4096, 6121, 8192):
             if n_up > nb and remaining > 70.0:
                y0 = resize_to_n(best_y, n_up)
                y0 = normalize_to_sum(y0, n_up)
                Lp, pp = get_fft_params(n_up)
                max_sec = min(remaining * 0.5, 180.0)
                yb, sb = optimize_runtime(y0, n_up, Lp, pp, hard_deadline, max_sec, rng, stop_score=1.35, give_up_seconds=25.0)
                if np.isfinite(sb) and sb < best_score:
                   best_score=float(sb); best_y=yb.copy(); print
                remaining = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
                break
    if best_y is None:
       best_y = np.ones(512, dtype=float)
       best_score = 2.0
    final = np.asarray(best_y, dtype=float).ravel()
    final = np.clip(final, 0.0, 1000.0)
    ssum = float(final.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(ssum) or ssum <= 0.01:
       final = np.ones(512, dtype=float)
    else:
       # scale to sum len(final)? scale invariant, but ensure sum >0.01. Leave or normalize?
       # Normalizing to sum len doesn't change score, but if values huge? okay.
       target = float(final.size)
       final = final * (target / ssum)
       final = np.clip(final, 0.0, 1000.0)
       # after clip re-normalize
       s2=float(final.sum())
       if s2 > 0: final = final*(target/s2)
    out = [float(x) for x in final]
    # optionally validate with evaluate_sequence
    try:
       ev = evaluate_sequence(out)
       if not np.isfinite(ev):
          # fallback uniform
          out = [1.0]*512
    except Exception:
       pass
    print(f"[final] n={len(out)} local_score={best_score:.6f}", flush=True)
    return out
```

Reverse sequence r[t]=f[n-1-t] for 0<=t<n else 0. R[k]=sum_{t=0}^{n-1} f[n-1-t] exp(-2j pi k t/L). Let j=n-1-t => t=n-1-j. R[k]=sum_j f[j] exp(-2j pi k (n-1-j)/L)=exp(-2j pi k (n-1)/L) sum_j f[j] exp(2j pi k j/L)=exp(-...)*conj(F[k]). Correct. Convolution C = r * w, DFT C = R * W. Good. Index p=i+n-1. ```python
"""
Nonnegative step-function coefficient optimizer.

Method summary
--------------
The objective is scale invariant:
    score(a) = 2 n max_k conv(a,a)_k / (sum a)^2.

We work with y = n * a / sum(a), so sum(y)=n and score = 2 max(conv(y,y)) / n.
A projected-gradient search is performed on the nonnegative simplex {y>=0, sum=n}.

At each step the vector y gives c = conv(y,y).  Lag weights are chosen from the
near-maximum convolution lags (an adaptive p-norm weight plus a small top-k blend).
The subgradient-like direction is
    2 sum_k w_k y_{k-i},
computed by FFT as a banded correlation.  A small anti-pairing correction is also
added for the top lags: for i+j=k, push mass toward the larger of y_i, y_j, since
this reduces the quadratic contribution on that lag in second order.

The direction is projected back onto the simplex and accepted by exact backtracking
on the maximum convolution coefficient.  Stagnation triggers random smooth,
antisymmetric, or block perturbations.  The search runs on several lengths and keeps
the best exact sequence found.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

TARGET_SCORE = 1.5027
MAX_VALUE = 1000.0


def get_global_height():
    name = "height_sequence_1"
    if name in globals():
        return globals()[name]
    return None


def next_fft_length(n):
    need = max(1, 3 * int(n) - 2)
    L = 1
    while L < need:
        L <<= 1
    return L


def make_phase(n, L):
    n = int(n)
    L = int(L)
    return np.exp(-2j * np.pi * float(n - 1) * np.arange(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L))


def get_fft_params(n):
    L = next_fft_length(n)
    phase = make_phase(n, L)
    return L, phase


def normalize_to_sum(x, target):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
    x = np.minimum(x, MAX_VALUE)

    target = float(target)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        x = np.ones(x.size, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(x.size)

    x = x * (target / s)
    x = np.minimum(x, MAX_VALUE)
    s2 = float(x.sum())
    if s2 <= 1e-15:
        x = np.ones(x.size, dtype=np.float64)
        s2 = float(x.size)
    x = x * (target / s2)
    return x


def resize_to_n(seq, n):
    n = int(n)
    seq = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = seq.size
    if m == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    seq = np.where(np.isfinite(seq), seq, 0.0)
    if m == n:
        return seq
    if m == 1:
        return np.full(n, float(seq[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m, endpoint=False, dtype=np.float64)
    new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False, dtype=np.float64)
    return np.interp(new, old, seq, dtype=np.float64)


def max_convolution(y, n, L):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return 0.0
    fy = np.fft.rfft(y, L)
    cf = np.fft.irfft(fy * fy, L)
    c = cf[: 2 * n - 1]
    if c.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    v = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(v):
        return 0.0
    return max(0.0, v)


def conv_and_F(y, n, L):
    n = int(n)
    F = np.fft.fft(y, L)
    C = np.fft.ifft(F * F)
    c = C[: 2 * n - 1].real
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c, F


def make_weights(c, p, top_blend=0.35):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    m = c.size
    if m == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    H = float(np.max(c))
    if H <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(m, 1.0 / m, dtype=np.float64)

    cn = c / H
    p = float(max(0.5, p))
    w = np.power(cn, p)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return w
    w /= s

    topk = min(8, m)
    if topk == m:
        idx = np.arange(m)
    elif topk == 1:
        idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))], dtype=int)
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(c, topk - 1)[-topk:]

    wtop = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    wtop[idx] = 1.0 / float(topk)
    w = (1.0 - float(top_blend)) * w + float(top_blend) * wtop
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return wtop
    w /= s
    return w


def correlation_weighted(F, w, wpad, L, phase, n):
    n = int(n)
    m = w.size
    wpad[:m] = w
    # The remainder of wpad has remained zero from initialization.
    W = np.fft.fft(wpad)
    Frev = np.conj(F) * phase
    C = np.fft.ifft(Frev * W)
    return 2.0 * C[n - 1: 2 * n - 1].real


def project_simplex(v, target):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = v.size
    if n <= 0:
        return v
    target = float(target)

    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    css = np.cumsum(u)
    k = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    cond = u - css / k >= -1e-12
    idx = np.where(cond)[0]
    if idx.size:
        rho = int(idx[-1])
        theta = (float(css[rho]) - target) / float(rho + 1)
    else:
        theta = (float(np.sum(v)) - target) / float(n)

    x = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        x = np.full(n, target / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
        return x

    x *= target / s
    x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
    s2 = float(x.sum())
    if s2 > 0.0:
        x *= target / s2
    return x


def smooth_noise(rng, n, smooth=1.0):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    smooth = float(smooth)
    sig = 0.02 + 0.06 * max(0.05, smooth)
    freq = np.fft.rfftfreq(n)
    size = n // 2 + 1
    spec = (rng.normal(size=size) + 1j * rng.normal(size=size)).astype(np.complex128)

    nz = freq > 1e-15
    scale = np.ones(size, dtype=np.float64)
    if np.any(nz):
        scale[nz] = np.exp(-0.5 * (freq[nz] / sig) ** 2)

    spec *= scale
    spec[0] = 0.0
    if n % 2 == 0:
        spec[-1] = 0.0

    z = np.fft.irfft(spec, n)
    z -= z.mean()
    std = float(np.std(z))
    if std <= 1e-12:
        z = rng.normal(size=n)
        z -= z.mean()
        std = float(np.std(z))
    if std > 1e-12:
        z /= std
    return np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)


def block_perturb(y, n, rng, sigma=None):
    n = int(n)
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if n < 3:
        return y.copy()

    if sigma is None:
        sigma = 0.15 + 0.35 * float(rng.random())

    cand = y.copy()
    min_width = max(2, n // 64)
    max_width = max(min_width + 1, n // 8)
    if max_width >= n:
        max_width = n

    width = int(rng.integers(min_width, max_width + 1))
    start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
    factor = float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma)))
    cand[start:start + width] *= factor

    if rng.random() < 0.25:
        width2 = max(2, width // 3)
        if width2 < n:
            start2 = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width2 + 1)))
            factor2 = float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma)))
            cand[start2:start2 + width2] *= factor2

    return normalize_to_sum(cand, n)


def add_random_direction(y, n, rng, amp):
    n = int(n)
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    r = float(rng.random())
    if r < 0.30:
        return block_perturb(y, n, rng)

    u = smooth_noise(rng, n, smooth=0.3 + 1.7 * float(rng.random()))
    if float(rng.random()) < 0.5:
        u = u - u[::-1]

    z = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64) + float(amp) * u
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, y)
    return project_simplex(z, n)


def make_initial(n, rng, base, kind, amp):
    n = int(n)
    amp = float(amp)

    if base is not None:
        base = np.asarray(base, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if base.size != n:
            base = resize_to_n(base, n)
        base = normalize_to_sum(base, n)

        if kind == 0:
            return base.copy()
        if kind == 1:
            u = smooth_noise(rng, n, smooth=0.5 + 1.5 * float(rng.random()))
            if float(rng.random()) < 0.5:
                u = u - u[::-1]
            y = base + amp * u
            return project_simplex(normalize_to_sum(y, n), n)

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False, dtype=np.float64)
    y = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    if kind == 2:
        u = smooth_noise(rng, n, smooth=0.5 + 2.0 * float(rng.random()))
        if float(rng.random()) < 0.5:
            u = u - u[::-1]
        y = y + amp * u

    elif kind == 3:
        R = 8 * (2 ** int(rng.integers(0, 4)))
        heights = np.exp(rng.normal(loc=0.0, scale=0.5, size=R)) + 0.3
        old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, R, endpoint=False, dtype=np.float64)
        y = np.interp(x, old, heights, dtype=np.float64)

    elif kind == 4:
        for _ in range(3):
            m = int(rng.integers(1, 16))
            ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            wa = float(rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0))
            y = y + amp * wa * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * float(m) * x + ph)

    elif kind == 5:
        u = smooth_noise(rng, n, smooth=0.8 + 0.8 * float(rng.random()))
        if float(rng.random()) < 0.5:
            u = u - u[::-1]
        y = y + amp * u

    elif kind == 6:
        u = smooth_noise(rng, n, smooth=0.3 + 1.7 * float(rng.random()))
        y = y * np.exp(np.minimum(5.0, np.maximum(-5.0, amp * u)))

    y = np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, 1.0)
    y = np.maximum(y, 0.0)
    return project_simplex(normalize_to_sum(y, n), n)


def optimize_runtime(y0, n, L, phase, hard_deadline, max_seconds, rng,
                     eta_start=0.20, stop_score=1.42, give_up_seconds=20.0):
    n = int(n)
    start = time.monotonic()
    end = min(start + max(1.0, float(max_seconds)), float(hard_deadline))

    y = normalize_to_sum(y0, n)
    c, F = conv_and_F(y, n, L)
    H = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    score = 2.0 * H / float(n)

    best_y = y.copy()
    best_score = score

    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    wpad = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    eta = float(eta_start)
    stuck = 0
    last_progress = start

    p_cycle = (4.0, 6.0, 8.0, 10.0, 13.0, 16.0, 20.0, 26.0)
    p_idx = 0

    while time.monotonic() < end:
        if not np.isfinite(score) or H <= 0.0:
            if best_y is not None:
                y = best_y.copy()
                c, F = conv_and_F(y, n, L)
                H = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
                score = 2.0 * H / float(n)
            else:
                break

        if time.monotonic() - last_progress > max(2.0, float(give_up_seconds)):
            break
        if score < float(stop_score):
            break

        p = p_cycle[p_idx]
        p_idx = (p_idx + 1) % len(p_cycle)

        w = make_weights(c, p)
        g = correlation_weighted(F, w, wpad, L, phase, n)
        g = np.asarray(g, dtype=np.float64)

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            direction = smooth_noise(rng, n, 1.0)
            direction = direction - float(np.mean(direction))
            rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(direction * direction)))
            if rms > 1e-12:
                direction /= rms

        else:
            g = g - float(np.mean(g))
            m = 0.70 * m + 0.30 * g
            direction = -m

            if c.size > 0 and float(rng.random()) < 0.75:
                topk = min(3, c.size)
                if topk > 1 and topk < c.size:
                    idx = np.argpartition(c, topk - 1)[-topk:]
                elif topk == 1:
                    idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))], dtype=int)
                else:
                    idx = np.arange(c.size)

                extra = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                for kk in idx:
                    k = int(kk)
                    lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
                    hi = min(n - 1, k)
                    if hi >= lo:
                        iarr = np.arange(lo, hi + 1)
                        jarr = k - iarr
                        mask = iarr < jarr
                        ii = iarr[mask]
                        jj = jarr[mask]
                        if ii.size:
                            # Push mass toward the larger of the two paired values.
                            diff = y[ii] - y[jj]
                            extra[ii] += diff
                            extra[jj] -= diff
                direction += 0.60 * extra

            direction = direction - float(np.mean(direction))
            rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(direction * direction)))
            if not np.isfinite(rms) or rms <= 1e-12:
                direction = smooth_noise(rng, n, 1.0)
                direction = direction - float(np.mean(direction))
                rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(direction * direction)))

            if np.isfinite(rms) and rms > 1e-12:
                direction /= rms
            else:
                direction = smooth_noise(rng, n, 1.0)
                direction = direction - float(np.mean(direction))

        trial = eta
        improved = False
        for _ in range(5):
            z = y + trial * direction
            new_y = project_simplex(z, n)
            Hn = max_convolution(new_y, n, L)
            sn = 2.0 * Hn / float(n)
            if np.isfinite(sn) and sn < score - 1e-9:
                y = new_y
                H = Hn
                score = sn
                if sn < best_score:
                    best_score = sn
                    best_y = y.copy()
                last_progress = time.monotonic()
                improved = True
                eta = min(eta * 1.12, 0.90)
                break
            trial *= 0.5

        if improved:
            c, F = conv_and_F(y, n, L)
            H2 = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
            score2 = 2.0 * H2 / float(n)
            H = H2
            score = score2
            if score2 < best_score:
                best_score = score2
                best_y = y.copy()
            stuck = 0
        else:
            eta = max(eta * 0.5, 1e-4)
            stuck += 1

            if stuck > 25 or time.monotonic() - last_progress > 10.0:
                score_before = score
                if float(rng.random()) < 0.25:
                    y = best_y.copy()
                y = add_random_direction(y, n, rng, 0.15 + 0.45 * float(rng.random()))
                c, F = conv_and_F(y, n, L)
                H = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
                score = 2.0 * H / float(n)

                if np.isfinite(score) and (score < score_before or score < best_score):
                    if score < best_score:
                        best_score = score
                        best_y = y.copy()
                    last_progress = time.monotonic()

                m.fill(0.0)
                stuck = 0
                eta = float(eta_start)

    return best_y, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    """
    Search for a nonnegative sequence minimizing evaluate_sequence().

    Returns a list of nonnegative floats.  The routine is time-bounded and always
    returns the best sequence found before the deadline.
    """
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 1800.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 1.0:
        budget = 1800.0

    start_global = time.monotonic()
    hard_deadline = start_global + max(2.0, budget - 15.0)

    try:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) + 1)
    except Exception:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(20260101)

    best_y = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    # Optional starting construction.
    base_seq = None
    g = get_global_height()
    if g is not None:
        try:
            arr = np.asarray(g, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
            if arr.size > 0:
                if arr.size > 12000:
                    base_seq = resize_to_n(arr, 6121)
                    base_n = 6121
                elif arr.size < 128:
                    base_seq = resize_to_n(arr, 256)
                    base_n = 256
                else:
                    base_seq = arr.copy()
                    base_n = int(arr.size)

                base_seq = normalize_to_sum(base_seq, base_n)
                s = 2.0 * max_convolution(base_seq, base_n, next_fft_length(base_n)) / float(base_n)
                if np.isfinite(s):
                    best_y = base_seq.copy()
                    best_score = float(s)
                    print(f"[init] global length={base_n} score={best_score:.6f}", flush=True)
        except Exception:
            base_seq = None

    # Build phases. If the supplied construction has a nonstandard length, include it.
    raw_phases = []
    standard = [
        (512, 0.24),
        (1024, 0.24),
        (2048, 0.18),
        (4096, 0.14),
        (6121, 0.12),
    ]

    if base_seq is not None:
        gb = int(base_seq.size)
        if gb not in [int(x[0]) for x in standard]:
            raw_phases.append((gb, 0.08))

    for n, frac in standard:
        if not any(int(p[0]) == int(n) for p in raw_phases):
            raw_phases.append((n, frac))

    total_frac = sum(float(f) for _, f in raw_phases)
    if total_frac <= 0.0:
        raw_phases = [(512, 1.0)]
        total_frac = 1.0

    available = max(60.0, hard_deadline - time.monotonic() - 15.0)
    phase_work = available * 0.90

    for n, frac in raw_phases:
        remaining = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
        if remaining < 20.0:
            break

        phase_budget = min((float(frac) / total_frac) * phase_work, max(0.0, remaining - 10.0))
        if phase_budget < 15.0:
            break

        n = int(n)
        L, ph = get_fft_params(n)

        starts = max(1, int(phase_budget / 35.0))
        starts = min(starts, 12)
        per_start = phase_budget / float(starts)

        if base_seq is not None:
            task_base = resize_to_n(base_seq, n)
            task_base = normalize_to_sum(task_base, n)
        else:
            task_base = None

        for si in range(starts):
            if time.monotonic() > hard_deadline - 10.0:
                break

            if task_base is not None and si == 0:
                kind = 0
            elif task_base is not None and si == 1:
                kind = 1
            else:
                kind = 2 + (si % 5)

            amp = 0.25 + 0.25 * ((si % 5) / 4.0) + 0.15 * float(rng.random())
            y0 = make_initial(n, rng, task_base, kind, amp)

            stop_s = 1.40 if best_score < TARGET_SCORE else 1.46
            give = min(20.0, max(5.0, 0.6 * per_start))
            max_sec = min(per_start + 5.0, max(5.0, hard_deadline - time.monotonic() - 10.0))

            yb, sb = optimize_runtime(
                y0, n, L, ph, hard_deadline, max_sec, rng,
                eta_start=0.20, stop_score=stop_s, give_up_seconds=give
            )

            if np.isfinite(sb) and sb < best_score:
                best_score = float(sb)
                best_y = np.asarray(yb, dtype=np.float64).copy()
                print(
                    f"[search] n={n} start={si} local={sb:.6f} best={best_score:.6f} "
                    f"time_left={hard_deadline - time.monotonic():.1f}",
                    flush=True
                )

    # Extra time: continue refining the current best.
    if best_y is not None:
        remaining = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
        if remaining > 45.0:
            nb = int(best_y.size)
            Lb, phb = get_fft_params(nb)
            stop_s = 1.32 if best_score < 1.45 else 1.38
            max_sec = min(remaining * 0.55, 360.0)
            yb, sb = optimize_runtime(
                best_y, nb, Lb, phb, hard_deadline, max_sec, rng,
                eta_start=0.22, stop_score=stop_s, give_up_seconds=30.0
            )
            if np.isfinite(sb) and sb < best_score:
                best_score = float(sb)
                best_y = np.asarray(yb, dtype=np.float64).copy()
                print(f"[extra] refined n={nb} score={best_score:.6f}", flush=True)

        # Upscaling can sometimes exploit a better discrete resolution.
        remaining = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
        if best_y is not None and remaining > 90.0:
            nb = int(best_y.size)
            tried = False
            for n_up in (4096, 6121, 8192):
                if n_up > nb and remaining > 70.0:
                    y0 = resize_to_n(best_y, n_up)
                    y0 = normalize_to_sum(y0, n_up)
                    Lp, pp = get_fft_params(n_up)
                    max_sec = min(remaining * 0.5, 180.0)
                    yb, sb = optimize_runtime(
                        y0, int(n_up), Lp, pp, hard_deadline, max_sec, rng,
                        eta_start=0.20, stop_score=1.35, give_up_seconds=25.0
                    )
                    if np.isfinite(sb) and sb < best_score:
                        best_score = float(sb)
                        best_y = np.asarray(yb, dtype=np.float64).copy()
                        print(f"[upscale] n={n_up} score={best_score:.6f}", flush=True)
                    tried = True
                    remaining = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
                    break
            if not tried:
                remaining = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
                if remaining > 60.0:
                    nb = int(best_y.size)
                    Lb, phb = get_fft_params(nb)
                    max_sec = min(remaining * 0.8, 180.0)
                    yb, sb = optimize_runtime(
                        best_y, nb, Lb, phb, hard_deadline, max_sec, rng,
                        eta_start=0.20, stop_score=1.32, give_up_seconds=30.0
                    )
                    if np.isfinite(sb) and sb < best_score:
                        best_score = float(sb)
                        best_y = np.asarray(yb, dtype=np.float64).copy()
                        print(f"[extra2] refined n={nb} score={best_score:.6f}", flush=True)

    if best_y is None:
        best_y = np.ones(512, dtype=np.float64)
        best_score = 2.0

    final = np.asarray(best_y, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    final = np.where(np.isfinite(final), final, 0.0)
    final = np.maximum(final, 0.0)
    final = np.minimum(final, MAX_VALUE)

    s_final = float(final.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s_final) or s_final <= 0.01:
        final = np.ones(512, dtype=np.float64)

    # Keep the returned sequence on the same scale convention, which does not change score.
    target = float(final.size)
    s_final = float(final.sum())
    if s_final > 0.0:
        final = final * (target / s_final)
        final = np.minimum(final, MAX_VALUE)
        s_final2 = float(final.sum())
        if s_final2 > 0.0:
            final = final * (target / s_final2)

    out = [float(x) for x in final]
    print(
        f"[final] n={len(out)} best_local_score={float(best_score):.6f} "
        f"time_used={time.monotonic() - start_global:.1f}s",
        flush=True
    )
    return out
```
